# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-17)

## Corpus Check
- 3879 files · ~9,979,532 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 95 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 30, .css 15, .entitlements 8)

## Summary
- 59056 nodes · 164942 edges · 1590 communities (1126 shown, 464 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6289 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `edc145aa`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).

## Import Cycles
- 2-file cycle: `Commander/crates/commander-indexer/src/lib.rs -> Commander/crates/commander-indexer/src/resources.rs -> Commander/crates/commander-indexer/src/lib.rs`
- 3-file cycle: `remix/app/components/Feed/MentionAutocomplete.tsx -> remix/app/components/Profile/ProfilePage.tsx -> remix/app/components/Feed/PostComposer.tsx -> remix/app/components/Feed/MentionAutocomplete.tsx`
- 3-file cycle: `remix/app/Providers/ThingtimeProvider.tsx -> remix/app/hooks/useThingtimeMachine.tsx -> remix/app/components/Thingtime/useThingtime.tsx -> remix/app/Providers/ThingtimeProvider.tsx`
